# Agents · L · part 1

893 entries, l to language-specialist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **l**: 专门审查物理推导与解答，检查跳步、错误近似、量纲问题、边界条件遗漏、符号定义不清和概念混淆。 ([zzr2718281828/claude-physics](https://github.com/zzr2718281828/claude-physics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/l.md) · ★1)
- **l**: Writes tests first against the plan's acceptance criteria using ecc's tdd-workflow, then later evaluates Gru's implementation against those tests and writes test-report.md (pass/fail verdict) and evaluation-report.md (broader evaluation against the plan's acceptance criteria). ([md-tonmoy007/software-factory](https://github.com/md-tonmoy007/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/l.md))
- **l-and-d-specialist**: Activate when the company needs durable ownership over program delivery, curriculum authoring, learning assessment, and adoption reinforcement. L&D Specialist converts recurring work in this lane into explicit artifacts, operating rules, and measurable decisions. ([einstenrodriguesdev/drax-plugin](https://github.com/einstenrodriguesdev/drax-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/drax/org/agents/l-and-d-specialist.md) · 2 copies)
- **l-lawliet**: The data analyst — cleans and reconciles messy data, decodes the pattern, and says what is actually true. Use to profile a dirty dataset, dedupe and reconcile conflicting sources, answer questions from the numbers, and separate the real signal from noise. States only what the evidence supports, and how confident it is. Never fabricates a finding to fill a gap. ([takoyaro/the-agency](https://github.com/takoyaro/the-agency/blob/HEAD/plugins/l-lawliet/agents/l-lawliet.md) · ★1)
- **l0-lead**: L0 Lead Agent. Program lead. Use for the daily digest, the weekly report, the review queue, gate status, risk register and escalations. Reports to Kireeti. ([KIREETI001/sewsure](https://github.com/KIREETI001/sewsure/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/l0-lead.md))

- **l1-agents** ([omarcelomoro/openclaw-backup](https://github.com/omarcelomoro/openclaw-backup/blob/HEAD/workspace/agents/l1-agents.md))
- **l1-client-suite-port-spec**: - **Date:** 2026-08-04 - **Status:** PROPOSED. - **Goal (definition of done):** kardamom's CI adopts the test-suite layers and harness patterns that L1 Ethereum execution clients (reth, geth, nethermind, besu, erigon) have converged on, wherever they map onto our architecture. Concretely: (1) the EEST state-test corpus (O(10⁴) EVM cases) runs in-process against `kardamom-engine` on every PR, pinne ([junemartes/kardamom](https://github.com/junemartes/kardamom/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/l1-client-suite-port-spec.md))
- **L1-doc-contract**: Ledger class: `doc-contract`. You own one unit per exported gadget (`gadget:&lt;package&gt;.&lt;Func&gt;|doc-contract`). ([shramee/zk-audit](https://github.com/shramee/zk-audit/blob/HEAD/plugins/zk-audit/skills/zk-audit/references/agents/L1-doc-contract.md))
- **l1-forger**: Level 1 — The Forger, the judgment executor of the inception workflow. Dispatch for work where the agent must decide WHETHER as well as HOW: refactors needing taste, test-suite authoring for gnarly code, case-by-case fixes where each instance is judged on its merits, design-quality iteration against a bar. Give it a fenced brief (task + why, files it must NOT touch, verification commands, required report format). Do not use for work whose outcome the prompt can fully specify — that's l2-point-man's job and a waste of this level. ([XyndoX/inception](https://github.com/XyndoX/inception/blob/HEAD/agents/l1-forger.md) · ★1)
- **l1-game-ux-checker**: L1_game_ux harness 独立质检 agent。沙箱化、只读权限，对指定阶段产物按客观规则跑 block/warn 检查，输出结构化 JSON 报告。不写任何文件——结果由主 Claude 写回 state.json。 ([linkang-agent-dot/ADHD_agent](https://github.com/linkang-agent-dot/ADHD_agent/blob/HEAD/_global_backup/claude-snapshot/agents/l1-game-ux-checker.md))
- **l1-intent**: Layer 1 (Intent and Context) agent for layered justifiable engagements. Establishes why the work exists, for whom, the success criteria, risk boundary and authority; issues the contract to Layer 2; reviews Layer 2 fragments; and answers the engagement's requirement upward to the human authority. Use only when the layered-justifiable-delivery skill dispatches this layer, never for a single-step task. ([leogalambos/JAI](https://github.com/leogalambos/JAI/blob/HEAD/obsolete/.ai/agents/l1-intent.md) · ★1)
- **l1-origin-deposit-derivation-spec**: - **Date:** 2026-07-25 - **Status:** Phases A to E landed. Epochs are the ONLY deposit path: the da-watcher emits one per finalized L1 block, `DepositRef` is retired, and a chain rebuilt from L1 with `kardamom-reconstruct --lockbox` includes its deposits - **Motivated by:** the chain-semantics suite's S8 (`docs/agents/chain-semantics-e2e-suite-spec.md`), which must keep its workload **deposit-free ([junemartes/kardamom](https://github.com/junemartes/kardamom/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/l1-origin-deposit-derivation-spec.md))
- **l1-reviewer**: Per-commit L1 code-quality reviewer — null safety, resource leaks, test quality, style. Produces structured Critical/Important/Minor findings with file:line cites. Used by /exloom at its review step for every Tier-1-or-higher change; do not start it directly. ([Expeed-Software/exloom](https://github.com/Expeed-Software/exloom/blob/HEAD/plugins/exloom/agents/l1-reviewer.md))
- **l1-support**: First line. Classifies a support issue, answers from the docs when the answer exists, asks for the missing redacted information, and escalates to L2 with a clear reason. ([RASMiranda/career-ai](https://github.com/RASMiranda/career-ai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/l1-support.md))
- **l1-support**: Специалист первой линии техподдержки. Вызывать, когда нужно разобрать очередь заявок - определить суть обращения, переклассифицировать, ответить пользователю по базе знаний, эскалировать инфраструктурный инцидент на L2 или завести баг-репорт в разработку. Примеры запросов - «обработай 5 новых заявок», «разбери очередь», «что с тикетом 42». ([ZoNNeRon/L1-support-AI-agent](https://github.com/ZoNNeRon/L1-support-AI-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/l1-support.md))
- **l10n**: Adds or changes localisation keys and languages by following the repo's own localisation recipe, then runs the repo's parity check ([Im5tu/claude](https://github.com/Im5tu/claude/blob/HEAD/agents/l10n.md) · ★6)
- **l10n-agent**: Diten yerelleştirme uzmanı: .resx kaynak dosyalarını oluşturur ve günceller (Platform modülü en+tr, kiracı modülü 7 dil), SharedResource ile sayfaya özel kaynak ayrımını ve window.L10n köprüsünü uygular. Kullanıcıya görünen metin eklenen ya da çevirisi eksik ekranlarda kullan. Backend, güvenlik, test ya da genel kod incelemesi için KULLANMA. ([bestepullukcu-alt/ERP-vNext](https://github.com/bestepullukcu-alt/ERP-vNext/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/l10n-agent.md))
- **l10n-agent**: Diten ERP vNext Localization (Çoklu Dil) uzmanı. Platform (2 dil) / Tenant (7 dil) ayrımına göre .resx dosya senkronizasyonu, SharedResource yönetimi ve Frontend (JavaScript) window.L10n köprüsü kurulumundan sorumludur. İnisiyatif almaz, kurallara uyar. ([bestepullukcu-alt/ERP-vNext](https://github.com/bestepullukcu-alt/ERP-vNext/blob/HEAD/.antigravity/agents/l10n-agent.md))
- **l10n-auditor**: Guarantees structural and linguistic integrity of 16 locale files (en, ko, ja, zh-CN, zh-TW, de, es, fr, pt, vi, ms, id, th, ru, it, ar) incl. RTL Arabic; runs the Vitest i18n harness after every key change; maintains the financial glossary. ([5throck/ai-workspace-standards](https://github.com/5throck/ai-workspace-standards/blob/HEAD/templates/co-price/agents/l10n-auditor.md) · ★25)
- **l10n-auditor**: 2026-08-25 ([5throck/ai-workspace-standards](https://github.com/5throck/ai-workspace-standards/blob/HEAD/templates/co-price/docs/lifecycle/agents/l10n-auditor.md) · ★25)
- **l10n-checker**: Verifies localization parity for this repo — every user-visible string must resolve through t(context, 'key') with both an `en` and a `da` entry in localizations.dart, and the two locale maps must stay in sync. Use PROACTIVELY after adding/changing UI strings or localization keys. Read-only. ([kasperstorm77/twelve_step_app](https://github.com/kasperstorm77/twelve_step_app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/l10n-checker.md) · ★1)
- **l10n-checker**: Prüft l10n-Konsistenz — ARB-Symmetrie zwischen `app_de.arb` und `app_en.arb`, Platzhalter-Sets, ARB-JSON-Validität und hardcodierte deutsche UI-Strings in `lib/`. Optionaler `--fix`-Modus ergänzt fehlende EN-Keys mit `[TODO en]`-Markern. ([KeRaMi-Assistant/inventory_management](https://github.com/KeRaMi-Assistant/inventory_management/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/l10n-checker.md))
- **l10n-engineer**: 현지화 판단 — 소스 문자열, 스토어 등재 텍스트, 이미지 안 텍스트, 폰트 스크립트 커버리지, 그리고 무엇을 번역하지 않을지. 트리거 "현지화", "로케일", "스토어 등재", "앱 제목", "두부", "폰트 커버리지", "ASO". ([snwlee/Nereus](https://github.com/snwlee/Nereus/blob/HEAD/plugins/nereus-l10n/agents/l10n-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **l10n-judge**: Pipeline-only QA judge of one applied translation batch: reads the l10n-batch/1 file that l10nkit batch export wrote and l10n-translate filled, and answers with one JSON object of MQM findings. Dispatched by l10n-translate. Not for translating, fixing text or writing notes. ([DoctorMozg/mozg-godot-pipelines](https://github.com/DoctorMozg/mozg-godot-pipelines/blob/HEAD/plugins/godot-l10n/agents/l10n-judge.md) · ★1)
- **l10n-pipeline-engineer**: Use for the localization pipeline: string extraction, TMS integration (Phrase, Lokalise, Crowdin, Transifex), translation memory, XLIFF/ARB/PO/JSON catalogs, and continuous localization. NOT for i18n architecture (i18n-architect) or QA/pseudo-localization gates (localization-qa-engineer). ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/localization-i18n-engineering/agents/l10n-pipeline-engineer.md) · ★9)

- **l2-point-man**: Level 2 — The Point Man, the mechanical executor of the inception workflow. - **l3-policy**: Layer 3 (Reasoning, Policy and Control) agent for layered justifiable engagements. Use only when the layered-justifiable-delivery skill dispatches this layer. ([leogalambos/JAI](https://github.com/leogalambos/JAI/blob/HEAD/obsolete/.ai/agents/l3-policy.md) · ★1)

- **l4-design**: Layer 4 (Solution Design) agent for layered justifiable engagements. - **l5-realisation**: Layer 5 (Realisation and Tooling) agent for layered justifiable engagements. Builds or configures the concrete artefacts a design calls for: code, infrastructure as code, pipeline definitions, container builds, prompts, configuration. The only layer agent that writes, and it writes only in the workspace. Derives evidence contracts for Layer 6 and reviews Layer 6 fragments. Use only when the layered-justifiable-delivery skill dispatches this layer. ([leogalambos/JAI](https://github.com/leogalambos/JAI/blob/HEAD/obsolete/.ai/agents/l5-realisation.md) · ★1)
- **l5-red-team-auditor**: Performs an uncompromising L5 Enterprise Red Team Audit on a given plugin against the 39-point architectural maturity matrix. - **l6-evidence**: Layer 6 (Execution and Evidence) agent for layered justifiable engagements. Executes deterministic checks and collects evidence: tests, scans, plans, queries, logs, configuration exports. Holds Bash but no Write or Edit: it has no file-editing tool, and the hooks and its instructions cover the shell. Returns observations with files the runtime hashes; never conclusions or recommendations. Use only when the layered-justifiable-delivery skill dispatches this layer. ([leogalambos/JAI](https://github.com/leogalambos/JAI/blob/HEAD/obsolete/.ai/agents/l6-evidence.md) · ★1)
- **l6-fidelity-auditor**: Use when built code must be verified against its Figma source all the way to L6 — and you want an HONEST score, not an optimistic one. ([Kahl-d/jaja](https://github.com/Kahl-d/jaja/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-hunt.md) · ★6)
- **lane-implementer**: Implementation lane with the full gate battery baked in - private target dir, all-targets clippy, targeted tests, provenance lint, commit-don't-push, residuals-to-issues reporting. Dispatch with opus for any fix/feature lane; the prompt then only needs the task itself. ([jamie8johnson/cqs](https://github.com/jamie8johnson/cqs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-implementer.md) · ★18)
- **lane-implementer**: Runs ONE GitHub issue end to end in a provisioned worktree (the GH-issue lane) — critically evaluate → fix with tests → open PR → CI green → merge it itself. Launched only by the main orchestrator with a context packet. Escalates any decision or finding above importance 2/5 rather than deciding alone. May delegate MECHANICAL execution to leaf subagents (code-implementer); never delegates judgment. ([spinje/pflow](https://github.com/spinje/pflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-implementer.md) · ★17)
- **lane-intake**: 총괄이 부른다. 입력 레인(F-I01 B(1~10))의 태스크 하나를 자기 worktree에서 구현하고 테스트·검사까지 끝낸 뒤 몇 줄로 보고한다. ([eunchaipark/SelfAudit](https://github.com/eunchaipark/SelfAudit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-intake.md))
- **lane-integrator**: Combines finished lane branches or worktrees into one local integration branch; returns conflict table and head SHA. ([marcioazam/nycode](https://github.com/marcioazam/nycode/blob/HEAD/packages/coding-agent/resources/agents/lane-integrator.md) · ★3)
- **lane-ios**: Builds one round's iOS part — iPhone, iPad and the Mac Catalyst app (apps/ios, native/geometry/swift) — in its own sibling worktree, to the settled spec and the round's acceptance list. Use for a round's iOS lane; the prompt gives the round, the spec and acceptance paths and the base branch. ([resonance-crew/Resonance](https://github.com/resonance-crew/Resonance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-ios.md) · ★1)
- **lane-lander**: Use to land one finished driver lane. Absorbed into the quarterback 2026-08-30. ([iasolb/claude-workbench](https://github.com/iasolb/claude-workbench/blob/HEAD/agents/lane-lander.md) · ★1)
- **lane-lander**: Lands a finished taskherd lane branch onto main and VERIFIES the merge actually carried the work. Use when a lane is done and its branch needs to become main, or when checking whether a previous land really landed. ([SpliFF/metalstorm](https://github.com/SpliFF/metalstorm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-lander.md))
- **lane-lead**: Owns one board item end to end in its own worktree: plan, build, review, integrate, hand to the merge gate. ([christian281150/AI-orchestrator_skill](https://github.com/christian281150/AI-orchestrator_skill/blob/HEAD/skills/build-orchestration-setup/assets/templates/.claude/agents/lane-lead.md) · ★1)
- **lane-lead**: Lead of ONE orchestration lane. Receives a brief written with the skill's template, builds it entirely, verifies it itself, and returns ONE receipt. Use it when a lane needs to launch as a subagent instead of as a named terminal. Does not coordinate other lanes, does not integrate, does not commit or push unless a permit is in force, and never merges. ([EsmehV99-code/orq](https://github.com/EsmehV99-code/orq/blob/HEAD/agents/lane-lead.md) · ★1)
- **lane-lead**: Leads one build lane (scanner, fixers, delivery, app UI, evidence pack, test harness). Use to split a lane's task into child jobs, run builders and reviewers, file their reports and report to the planner. ([harsh-2711/wideaisle](https://github.com/harsh-2711/wideaisle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-lead.md))
- **lane-live**: Worker for the live lane of an approved code plan - publishes the build through the profile's publish or live verb into the host session that is ALREADY open, runs the change on real data, reads the result back from the host and loops until the SPEC.md line holds. On a project whose profile declares live.loop it also runs the baseline task BEFORE the red test - ensure the host's MCP server is on, measure live, return the number as a baseline row. - **lane-logic**: Worker for the logic lane of an approved plan - only the unit task ids it is handed, only files inside their files globs, write tests from the contract table in a red turn or make locked tests green with the smallest code in a green turn, with no host running. ([Cazorlas/Paper.ScreenWizzard](https://github.com/Cazorlas/Paper.ScreenWizzard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-logic.md))
- **lane-manager**: Starts, resumes, parks and finishes lanes (branch + worktree + registry file) in the control plane. - **lane-manager**: Starts, resumes, parks and finishes lanes (branch + worktree + lane file) in the workspace. Use when the user says they want to start new work, switch to other work, leave for the day, or close something out. ([aj-oss-tools/repolane](https://github.com/aj-oss-tools/repolane/blob/HEAD/templates/claude/agents/lane-manager.md))
- **lane-medium**: Full-tool lane for clearly scoped work — copy edits, translations, spacing and alignment in shared components, small UI polish with an obvious right answer. Effort medium. ([rusyander/claude-control](https://github.com/rusyander/claude-control/blob/HEAD/apps/server/assets/kit/agentdeck-kit/agents/lane-medium.md))
- **lane-model**: Worker for the model lane of an approved model plan - changes the user's model or drawing in the host session that is already open, each group through survey, dry run with nothing kept, commit, and an independent read-back, every step citing the rule codes it builds to. Writes a rule the user states into the project's rule file the same turn, keeps every script as a rerunnable harness, reports leftovers by name. - **lane-orchestrator**: Runs ONE lane of a bd execution plan as an in-session orchestrator subagent under the super orchestrator (day mode). - **lane-posts**: The posts and people lane (charter section 5): each morning reads new hiring posts on LinkedIn (the owner's Chrome, read only) and X (official API, within its monthly budget cap) and the watch list; logs finds in state/posts/; picks people to follow; drafts comments and notes; acts only on the owner's yes. - **lane-prep**: The prep lane: gets the owner ready for every interview round from their real record (prep packs from the cited question bank and the STAR stories, scored mocks, debriefs), and keeps the bank and the stories current; never helps during a live round. - **lane-protocol**: Updated: 2026-11-12. This protocol tells a session how to work a wayfinder ticket from claim to close: read the ticket, build the smallest slice that satisfies it, verify, and record the result. - **lane-reconciler**: Reconciles ghost lanes — cases where lane manifests, Linear state, and GitHub PR/branch state have drifted apart. Use when ops:health reports drift or before starting a new dispatch cycle. ([griff843/Unit-Talk-v2](https://github.com/griff843/Unit-Talk-v2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-reconciler.md))
- **lane-recruiter**: The Lead Recruiter, head of the owner's job agency: runs the day (sweep, list, priorities, flashcards, quick actions, evening wrap), sets the standard for every lane, reviews their work, reverse engineers every company, role and person, and learns from every outcome. - **lane-relayer**: Carries ONE request to a partner lane (gemini, ccs) and returns its envelope verbatim. A courier for context isolation and fan-out: the lane skill has already built the command. - **lane-report**: Compose one developer lane's note, JSON report and pull request payload, then die with your context. - **lane-research**: The research lane: reverse-engineers one role at a time into Role Package v2 (lens, ranked coverage matrix, company signal, hiring-manager persona, people map, fit and visa) so the resume lane can present the owner's real work the way that reader needs. - **lane-resume**: The resume lane: produces the page for one role at a time from the base through pipeline v2 (role package, coverage matrix, draft, scripts, editor, blind base compare, four readers) and presents it to the owner with its diff. - **lane-reviewer**: Lane read-only reviewer for night_review / specialist. GitNexus + diffs. No product writes. Not a PM and not a writer. ([VKirill/claude-lane-stack](https://github.com/VKirill/claude-lane-stack/blob/HEAD/profiles/opencode/agents/lane-reviewer.md) · ★125)
- **lane-reviewer**: Generic adversarial reviewer for an implementation lane in any project — verifies the lane's claims against the real git diff (never the report), runs the checks it safely can, and returns SHIP / SHIP_WITH_FIXES with exact file:line fixes. ([iceteps/claude-settings-clean](https://github.com/iceteps/claude-settings-clean/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/lane-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **lane-reviewer**: Adversarial read-only reviewer for a lane of OspreyFlow's parallel build (ADR-142). Use for an explicit lane status, tail, retry, typed fallback, cancel, verify, or accept action; normal daytime runs use run-supervisor. ([VKirill/claude-lane-stack](https://github.com/VKirill/claude-lane-stack/blob/HEAD/plugins/lane-stack/agents/lane-supervisor.md) · ★125)
- **lane-tender**: Use to run the operational land/gate/close loop over every driver lane. Absorbed into the quarterback 2026-08-30. ([iasolb/claude-workbench](https://github.com/iasolb/claude-workbench/blob/HEAD/agents/lane-tender.md) · ★1)
- **lane-tester**: Runs one testing lane against a stamped application through lanes.py and reads back the report. Use for test tasks and for the run-lanes skill. ([never2average/software-factory](https://github.com/never2average/software-factory/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/lane-tester.md) · ★1)
- **lane-topology**: A multi-agent development pattern that sizes itself to the work: process is assigned **mechanically, before any work starts**, instead of every task running the same lifecycle. ([CowboyLogic/ai-dev](https://github.com/CowboyLogic/ai-dev/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lane-topology.md) · ★5)
- **lane-triage**: Picks the lane (express, standard, full, hotfix) when there is no design stage, from `lfg lane --json` plus the brief. Can only raise the suggested lane, never lower it. Used by /lfg, /fix and /wizard on small changes. ([varun-singhh/lfg](https://github.com/varun-singhh/lfg/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/lane-triage.md) · ★11)
- **lane-ui**: Worker for the UI lane of an approved plan - only the ui or e2e task ids it is handed, only files inside their files globs, windows, dialogs, pages and view models built on MOCK data, write tests from the contract table in a red turn or make locked tests green in a green turn, every screenshot opened and judged against the plan's wireframe, with no host running. Runs beside the logic lane but never beside lane-live, because both need the desktop. Returns one evidence row per task, commits once on its own worktree branch to hand the work back, and never ticks the plan or pushes. ([Cazorlas/Paper.ScreenWizzard](https://github.com/Cazorlas/Paper.ScreenWizzard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-ui.md))
- **lane-utilization**: From the repository root, query retained runs history for a time window and report observed lane capacity as versioned JSON: ([ashlrai/phantom](https://github.com/ashlrai/phantom/blob/HEAD/examples/agents/lane-utilization.md) · ★5)
- **lane-verifier**: Read-only hunter or verifier for the CI review lanes and for model diversity in the /review:fanout-sweep workflow: hunts candidate findings in one slice of a pull request or one change set, or tries to refute one candidate another agent produced, as its brief names. Dispatched by /review:code-review, /review:security-review and the fanout-sweep workflow's model tiers; not intended for direct ad-hoc use. ([melodic-software/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/melodic-software/claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/review/agents/lane-verifier.md) · ★32)
- **lane-web**: Builds one round's web part (Next.js under src/, and scripts/ when the round needs it) in its own sibling worktree, to the settled spec and the round's acceptance list. Use for a round's web lane; the prompt gives the round, the spec and acceptance paths and the base branch. ([resonance-crew/Resonance](https://github.com/resonance-crew/Resonance/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-web.md) · ★1)
- **lane-worker**: Default sub-agent for bounded implementation lanes (edit code, run checks, submit CI). Lean tool set, so it starts with a much smaller context than general-purpose. Use it for any lane that only needs to read, edit and run commands; use general-purpose only when the lane needs web access, skills or its own sub-agents. ([glowingkitty/OpenMates](https://github.com/glowingkitty/OpenMates/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-worker.md) · ★53)
- **lane-worker**: Isolated implementation lane for a commissioned parallel burn. Owns exactly one file-disjoint slice of a larger contract, follows the pilot/spec verbatim, checkpoints progress to AgentDB, and never commits. Spawned only when an orchestrator has genuinely independent, file-disjoint work to run concurrently. ([ariaxhan/kernel-claude](https://github.com/ariaxhan/kernel-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/lane-worker.md) · ★15)
- **lane-worker**: Does ONE task of a measurement lane — a capture to take, a verifier or the model to extend, a branch to stimulate — editing only the files its lane brief names. Returns claims with the verifier, capture and build that reproduce each. Never edits facts/, never commits. ([JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD](https://github.com/JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-worker.md) · ★6)
- **lane-worker**: Builds one planned task inside one lane of a parallel build, in its own git worktree. Give it a lane brief (task id, base sha, owned paths, acceptance criteria, tests, done-when). It works test-first, commits per step, stays inside its lane, and ends with a fixed-shape handoff. ([AnwarAndargie/productwright](https://github.com/AnwarAndargie/productwright/blob/HEAD/agents/lane-worker.md) · ★3)
- **lane-worker**: 실행 레인 sub-agent — tasks.md 를 파일 소유권으로 갈랐을 때 한 레인을 맡아 코드를 고쳐요. 소유 파일 목록 밖은 건드리지 않고, 커밋하지 않고, 주장엔 명령과 출력을 인용해요. 트리거: '실행 레인', 'lane worker', '이 파일들 맡아서', '레인 A 진행'. ([bluecheat/ax](https://github.com/bluecheat/ax/blob/HEAD/agents/lane-worker.md) · ★2)
- **lane-worker**: Background worker that builds ONE ready-for-agent ticket inside the current lane and commits it to its own worktree branch. Dispatched by the orchestrator chat (hack-orchestrate skill); never talks to the human, never ships. ([Jograh/hack-kit](https://github.com/Jograh/hack-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-worker.md) · ★1)
- **lane-worker**: L2 lane worker - implements exactly one lane contract inside an isolated git worktree, runs both controls, and returns a devloop_report. Dispatched by the orchestrator; not for direct use on unscoped tasks. ([MiOS-Developer/-dev-loop](https://github.com/MiOS-Developer/-dev-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/lane-worker.md) · ★1)
- **lane-worker**: Matching worker for one orchestrator lane in its own slot worktree. The orchestrator launches it with the lane prompt; it is not for ad-hoc tasks. ([Belorisa/STBC-DECOMP](https://github.com/Belorisa/STBC-DECOMP/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-worker.md))
- **lane-worker**: Executes one lane or patch batch from a dev-loop brief inside an assigned worktree and write set. Use for implementation, fixes and plan execution delegated by the orchestrator. Not for design or review. ([HuakunShen/skills](https://github.com/HuakunShen/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/agent-engineering/dev-loop/templates/agents/lane-worker.md))
- **lane-writer**: Lane conveyor implementer. One TASK_FILE, owns_paths only, LANE_REPORT. Use for adoc write / lane-session --agent. Not a PM. Do not run tests. ([VKirill/claude-lane-stack](https://github.com/VKirill/claude-lane-stack/blob/HEAD/profiles/opencode/agents/lane-writer.md) · ★125)
- **lane-writer**: Lane conveyor implementer. One TASK_FILE, owns_paths only, LANE_REPORT. Not a PM. Do not run tests. ([VKirill/claude-lane-stack](https://github.com/VKirill/claude-lane-stack/blob/HEAD/profiles/cursor/lane-writer/agents/lane-writer.md) · ★125)
- **lane-writer**: Single writer for one lane of a parallel workflow. Owns an explicit file list, never reaches outside it, and reports rather than improvising when the work needs a file it does not own. Use for every build lane in a multi-lane round. ([tom2025b/git-vista](https://github.com/tom2025b/git-vista/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lane-xhigh**: Full-tool lane for work where the path is still open — research, planning, task decomposition and structuring, design decisions, hard analysis, designing strict checks and verification scripts. Not for execution or review (→ lane-high). Effort xhigh. Spawn only after the user approved subagents for THIS task. ([rusyander/claude-control](https://github.com/rusyander/claude-control/blob/HEAD/apps/server/assets/kit/agentdeck-kit/agents/lane-xhigh.md))
- **lane3-drafter**: Drafts a livestream batch's ENTIRE Lane 3 (text + image) plan from its transcript: topic selection, fact-check, every tweet / one-liner / IG companion / YT poll / X poll / YT post + carousel slide prompts / thread, and every image prompt, then writes and VALIDATES `repurpose/output/&lt;batch&gt;-lane3-plan.json` (the handoff artifact the Wave 6 repurpose graph consumes). Consult for the Lane 3 judgment step of a livestream batch, or when the batch orchestrator needs Lane 3 drafted headlessly. Writes exactly ONE file. Generates no images, touches no queue, runs no graph. ([CodeMonkey-Mike/social-media](https://github.com/CodeMonkey-Mike/social-media/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lane3-drafter.md))
- **lanes**: A lane is one of the routes the same behaviour can travel: the main checkout or a linked worktree, a Claude install or a Codex install, dev or prod, a small fixture or real-size data. This document is the canonical statement of how a project lists its lanes, and of how a plan and a closing report say which lanes a claim was exercised in. Other surfaces point here; they do not restate it. ([cooneycw/claude-power-pack](https://github.com/cooneycw/claude-power-pack/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lanes.md) · ★21)
- **LANES**: Two managers work in this repo at the same time, each in its own lane. They coordinate in the pinned issue **"Lane board: web ⇄ native"** (#622). ([vllnt/ui](https://github.com/vllnt/ui/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/LANES.md) · ★13)
- **lanes**: How the implementers of a wave are kept apart, and how their work is merged. How the work is split is [delegation.md](delegation.md); how a wave is checked and committed is [checking.md](checking.md). ([canmi21/canmi](https://github.com/canmi21/canmi/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lanes.md) · ★1)
- **lanes**: Root `AGENTS.md` § "Review gate for behavior-changing work" lists the lanes in one line each. This page says what each lane's **role** does, and how a card moves through Review (BE-23 on the cloud, FE-118 in the desktop app). ([fuongz/lanework](https://github.com/fuongz/lanework/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lanes.md) · ★1)
- **lanes-and-releases**: Read this before you board the train, change a lane or a required check, or work on promotion or a release. `CLAUDE.md` holds the rules every session needs; this holds the detail. Nothing here widens them: an agent never pushes `main` but by the train, never sets `HECKS_OWNER_PUSH`, never uses `--no-verify`, and never writes a `Release-Approved-By` line on its own. ([heckslabs/hecks](https://github.com/heckslabs/hecks/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/lanes-and-releases.md))
